- **qrspi-design-critic-decision-readiness**: Internal QRSPI workflow agent — the non-producer DECISION-READINESS lens of the design-phase review panel. Terminal-advisory ONLY — it never drives a revise round. Spawned by the /review-design command (advisory, propose-only). Not for general code review. ([russelltsherman/qrspi](https://github.com/russelltsherman/qrspi/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/qrspi-design-critic-decision-readiness.md) · ★1)
- **qrspi-design-critic-design-review**: Internal QRSPI workflow agent — the adversarial NODE-VALIDITY lens of the design-phase critic panel (DESIGN-REVIEW). Reads the actual source under CODEBASE_PATH and judges whether the produced artifact is materially WRONG — unsound, incorrect against real code, or built on a claim the codebase contradicts — emitting a {pass, findings} verdict. Spawned by runCriticPanelLoop in qrspi-batch.js (opt-in, default-OFF). Not for general code review. ([russelltsherman/qrspi](https://github.com/russelltsherman/qrspi/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/qrspi-design-critic-design-review.md) · ★1)
- **qrspi-design-critic-edge-alignment**: Internal QRSPI workflow agent — the EDGE lens of the design-phase critic panel (TICKET/RESEARCH ALIGNMENT). Spawned by runCriticPanelLoop in qrspi-batch.js. Not for general code review. ([russelltsherman/qrspi](https://github.com/russelltsherman/qrspi/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/qrspi-design-critic-edge-alignment.md) · ★1)
- **qrspi-design-critic-internal-consistency**: Internal QRSPI workflow agent — one lens of the design-phase critic panel (INTERNAL CONSISTENCY).
